024858:   function updateRefineBtn(){
024859:     var btn = document.getElementById('cfRefineBtn');
024860:     if(!btn) return;
024861:     var result = window.state && window.state.simResult;
024862:     var isFacial = result && result.sourceKind === 'Facial';
024863:     if(isFacial && !result._secondaryRefined){
024864:       btn.style.display = '';
024865:       btn.disabled = false;
024866:       btn.style.opacity = '';
024867:       btn.innerHTML = '✦ Refine<br>精密生成';
024868:     } else {
024869:       btn.style.display = 'none';
024870:     }
024871:   }

024873:   // Gated refinement: 1) evaluate ROI/landmarks/imageType → 2) decide if a refine is safe →
024874:   // 3) run a CLAMPED full-frame pass → 4) soft-matte composite (no rectangle) →
024875:   // 5) quality check → 6) reject & keep the first-pass on any artifact, else adopt.
024876:   window.runSecondaryRefinement = async function(){
024877:     var btn = document.getElementById('cfRefineBtn');
024878:     var result = window.state && window.state.simResult;
024879:     var dbg = { imageType:null, secondaryRefineEnabled:false, secondaryRefineSkippedReason:null,
024880:       roiCore:null, roiSupport:null, roiContext:null, blendMode:null, seamScore:null,
024881:       outsideRegionChangeScore:null, artifactReject:null, finalSource:'first-pass' };
024882: 
024883:     if(!result || !result.dataUrl){ toast('生成結果が見つかりません'); return; }
024884:     if(!getAiEndpoint()){ toast('AI バックエンドが設定されていません'); return; }
024885: 
024886:     var imageType = (result.sourceKind==='Facial') ? 'facial' : 'focus';
024887:     dbg.imageType = imageType;
024888: 
024889:     if(btn){ btn.disabled=true; btn.innerHTML='…<br>処理中'; }
024890:     try{
024891:       var primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
024892:       var primImg = await loadImg(primaryDataUrl);
024893:       var w = primImg.naturalWidth||primImg.width, h = primImg.naturalHeight||primImg.height;
024894: 
024895:       // ── GATE: facial needs reliable mouth landmarks; without them, a safe local refine
024896:       //    is impossible → SKIP and keep the first-pass (never force a blind patch). ──
024897:       var poly = null;
024898:       if(imageType==='facial'){
024899:         poly = await getLipOuterRing(primImg, w, h);
024900:         if(!poly){
024901:           dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='no_facial_landmarks';
024902:           console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg);
024903:           if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
024904:           toast('顔のランドマークが安定しないため二次生成をスキップしました（一次結果を維持）');
024905:           return;
024906:         }
024907:       }
024908:       dbg.secondaryRefineEnabled = true;
024909: 
024910:       // ── CLAMPED full-frame refine pass (facial = identity-preserving, low denoise) ──
024911:       toast(imageType==='facial' ? 'スマイルバランスを control 補正中…' : 'ゴールデンプロポーション二次生成中…');
024912:       var refinedFull = await generateRefinedViaAI(primaryDataUrl, imageType);
024913: 
024914:       // ── Soft-matte composite (polygon/ellipse, never a rectangle) + quality metrics ──
024915:       toast('一次結果へなじませ中…');
024916:       var comp = await compositeRefinedSoft(primaryDataUrl, refinedFull, imageType, poly);
024917:       var m = comp.metrics||{};
024918:       dbg.roiCore=m.roiCore; dbg.roiSupport=m.roiSupport; dbg.roiContext=m.roiContext;
024919:       dbg.blendMode=m.blendMode; dbg.seamScore=m.seamScore;
024920:       dbg.outsideRegionChangeScore=m.outsideRegionChangeScore; dbg.artifactReject=!!m.artifactReject;
024921: 
024922:       if(m.artifactReject){
024923:         // ── FALLBACK: a worse/patchy result is never adopted — keep the first-pass. ──
024924:         dbg.finalSource='first-pass';
024925:         dbg.secondaryRefineSkippedReason='artifact_reject:'+(m.rejectCause||'unknown');
024926:         console.log('[CaseFlow Refine]', dbg, m);
024927:         if(btn){ btn.disabled=false; btn.innerHTML='✦ Refine<br>精密生成'; btn.style.opacity=''; }
024928:         toast('二次生成が不自然なため一次結果を維持しました');
024929:         return;
024930:       }
024931: 
024932:       // ── ADOPT the second-pass ──
024933:       dbg.finalSource='second-pass';
024934:       result._primaryDataUrl = result._primaryDataUrl || result.dataUrl;
024935:       result._secondaryRefined = true;

019470:       'In 2D dental photographs, overlapping or lingually displaced teeth cast DEEP SHADOWS between and behind adjacent teeth. ' +
019471:       'These shadows appear as dark gaps or black spaces in the image but are NOT open interdental spaces — they are optical depth effects caused by the 3-dimensional arch being compressed onto a flat sensor.\n'+

020606:   async function ensureIntraoralFlag(src){

020608:     // Resolve flags: intraoral (badge + enamel processing), intraoralDark (black-contraster
020609:     // subtype) and lips (whether lips/perioral tissue are present — gates lip/smile rendering).

020613:         // defaults to NO lips so the generator never paints lips onto a retracted view; the

018568:   /* ---- map simulator-page concept + complaint cards -> initial axis values ---- */
018569:   function deriveFromSelections(){
018570:     const v = state.simV82 || {};
018571:     const t = v.targets || {};
018572:     const concept = v.concept || 'ortho';
018573:     const on = k => (t[k] ? 50 : 0);                 // selected complaint => evidence-based
018574:     const s = {
018575:       // Shade is centred at 50 (neutral). A discolouration complaint implies the
018576:       // patient wants WHITENING, so it maps above centre; no complaint stays neutral.
018577:       shade:      t.shade ? 72 : 50,
018578:       alignment:  on('space'),
018579:       // Tooth Shape is bidirectional (50 = neutral; <50 tapering, >50 square). It starts
018580:       // neutral so the clinician chooses the form type on the slider.
018581:       toothShape: 50,
018582:       scallop:    on('scallop'),
018583:       smileFrame: on('harmony')
018584:     };
018585:     // Orthodontic concept => default baseline now sits in the IDEAL-finish band
018586:     // (≈85) for a clearly dynamic correction out of the box; the slider can still
018587:     // push to a fully resolved 100, or be eased back for a subtler result.
018588:     if(concept==='ortho'){      s.alignment=Math.max(s.alignment,85); s.smileFrame=Math.max(s.smileFrame,38); }
018589:     if(concept==='prosthetic'){ s.shade=Math.max(s.shade,66); }
018590:     if(concept==='hybrid'){     s.alignment=Math.max(s.alignment,80); s.shade=Math.max(s.shade,62); }
018591:     Object.keys(s).forEach(k=>s[k]=clamp(s[k],0,100));
018592:     return s;
018593:   }

018596:   function loadImage(src){

018599:   function rr(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){ ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);ctx.closePath(); }
018600:   function smoothstep(a,b,x){ const t=clamp((x-a)/(b-a),0,1); return t*t*(3-2*t); }

018602:   // Affine + triangle warp (clip + setTransform).
018603:   function affine(s0,s1,s2,d0,d1,d2){
018604:     const sx1=s1.x-s0.x,sy1=s1.y-s0.y,sx2=s2.x-s0.x,sy2=s2.y-s0.y,
018605:           dx1=d1.x-d0.x,dy1=d1.y-d0.y,dx2=d2.x-d0.x,dy2=d2.y-d0.y,det=sx1*sy2-sx2*sy1;
018606:     if(Math.abs(det)<1e-7) return null;
018607:     const a=(dx1*sy2-dx2*sy1)/det,c=(sx1*dx2-sx2*dx1)/det,e=d0.x-a*s0.x-c*s0.y,

019997:   async function fetchSimWithRetry(ep, body, maxAttempts){
019998:     maxAttempts=maxAttempts||4;
019999:     let lastErr=null;
020000:     for(let attempt=1; attempt<=maxAttempts; attempt++){
020001:       try{
020002:         const res=await fetch(ep,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:body});
020003:         if(res.ok) return res;
020004:         // Retry transient statuses; surface anything else immediately.
020005:         if(RETRY_STATUS[res.status] && attempt<maxAttempts){
020006:           let t=''; try{ t=await res.text(); }catch(e){}
020007:           lastErr=new Error('HTTP '+res.status+' '+t.slice(0,400));
020008:         }else{
020009:           let t=''; try{ t=await res.text(); }catch(e){}
020010:           throw new Error('HTTP '+res.status+' '+t.slice(0,400));
020011:         }
020012:       }catch(e){
020013:         // Network/abort errors are retryable too; rethrow non-retryable HTTP errors.
020014:         if(/^HTTP \d/.test(e&&e.message||'') && !RETRY_STATUS[parseInt((e.message.match(/HTTP (\d+)/)||[])[1],10)]) throw e;
020015:         lastErr=e;
020016:         if(attempt>=maxAttempts) break;
020017:       }
020018:       // Exponential backoff with jitter: ~1s, 2s, 4s (+0-400ms).
020019:       const wait=Math.pow(2,attempt-1)*1000 + Math.floor(Math.random()*400);
020020:       const msg='サーバー混雑のため再試行中… ('+attempt+'/'+(maxAttempts-1)+')';
020021:       try{
020022:         const subEl=document.querySelector('.cfWaitOverlay .cfWaitSub');
020023:         if(subEl) subEl.textContent=msg;
020024:         else if(typeof toastFn==='function') toastFn(msg);
020025:         else if(typeof toast==='function') toast(msg);
020026:       }catch(e){}
020027:       await sleep(wait);
020028:     }
020029:     throw lastErr || new Error('リクエストに失敗しました');
020030:   }
